# Parity status: Plan Studio vs Chief Architect X18

Snapshot of 2026-10-08 against HEAD 4c11b69 (Rounds 10-12 landed), re-audited after nine builders edited rows and totals by hand. Every area total below is counted from the per-id rows (a script over the detail tables, not an addition of earlier totals); every row still Partial or Missing was re-checked against the code (the feature or control named in its gap searched for in `crates/`, then the implementing tool, dialog or engine read; rows whose limit is a question about Chief's behavior rather than missing code were left to the verify list), and every Works row's cited files, functions and tests were checked to still exist. No Rust file was edited. The workspace was being edited by other builders during this pass. `cargo test -p plan-app scenarios` ran once the tree compiled: 208 passed and 1 failed, `s18_doors_windows_3d::bigger_flavors_have_more_geometry_than_the_plain_ones` (the Bay Window assertion, in the in-flight 3D opening work; DW-48 is Partial either way). The tests cited as evidence for the rows flipped in this pass were run by name where the tree allowed it.

**Status words.** Works: behavior is present and exercised (a test, a scenario or the tool itself). Partial: present with a stated limit. Missing: no code path or a dimmed/stub control. Differs-by-design: deliberately different (a DECISIONS.md call, the manual's "Known differences" or a spec note) so it is not counted as a gap to close. Statuses come from code reading plus the manual chapters; "verify in Chief" items are not settled by this table (see the list below).

There are 765 parity ids: W 105, S 112, DW 110, R 71, RF 60, DIM 45, TXT 19, CAD 42 (CAD-17..19 do not exist in the spec), LAY 15, C 71, CB 68, L 47. Stairs, framing, terrain, library and electrical are CB-22..CB-68; no separate parity spec exists for exterior details, slabs, images or schedules beyond L-23..L-32 (their status lives in manual chapters 16 and 17).

What the recount found. The previous area table already matched its rows except Cabinets (it said 15 Works and 6 Partial; the rows were 14 and 7), so the overall line was off by one (489 Works against 488 counted). The per-area headings inside the detail section and the Status line at the top of each `docs/parity/*.md` were the unreliable numbers (for example Doors/Windows said 70 Works and 16 Missing, Rooms said 45 Works, 3D said 31 Works and 14 Missing); both are rewritten from the rows. This pass then moved 51 rows (listed under "Changes in this pass" below): 47 up to Works (38 from Partial, 9 from Missing), 4 from Missing to Partial, none down. No Works row lost its evidence: the cited files and tests still exist; four rows cited names that had been renamed and now cite the current ones (W-88, DW-98, R-16, RF-16).

Reading the table: the engines are strong (walls, joins, roofs, stairs, terrain, 3D, layout engine, schedules). The remaining gaps cluster in the 3D menu and camera tooling, document layout details (page specification, templates, print preview), wall specification tabs (foundation, cap, covering, platform intersections), door and window plan details, room finish surfaces and the text box.

## "Verify in Chief" items for one live session

Consolidated by screen, for ids whose row did not change in this pass (the 14 ids that were also on the old list and were settled by code evidence (13 by this pass: W-45, W-93, W-105, S-18, S-28, S-40, S-41, DIM-37, CAD-25, RF-21, C-55, C-56, C-58; C-39 by the 3D builder) are gone). The nine "Lines marked (verify in Chief) ..." preamble sentences of the parity files are not counted. Where one id carries two markers, both are listed. The count after each heading is the number of markers on that screen. Total: 194 in `docs/parity`, 1 in `docs/integration-queue.md`, 1 in `DECISIONS.md` (#6) = **196** (the old list said 213: 14 settled ids are gone and two old headings had been miscounted, Cabinets-to-Library 12 for 10 and Dimension-Text-CAD 31 for 30).

### 1. Plan view: wall tools, snapping, joins (21)
- Drawing: W-1 interior wall default thickness; W-3 right-click does not end chain, double-click does; W-4 does drag-release continue the chain; W-5 closing on first point is a proper corner; W-14 alignment guide set; W-15 angle readout origin; W-16 Tab behavior in length/angle entry; W-18 Shift constrains to 0/90 or not; W-19 typed length rounding to Dimension Defaults.
- Sides and joins: W-21 exterior side is right or left of drawing direction; W-22 no auto-flip of layer stack after a room forms; W-34 3+ walls at a point pick the through wall; W-35 T-junction keeps the through wall whole (we split it); W-37 stepped faces when main layers differ in thickness.
- Repair and edit: W-42 Fix Wall Connections also removes duplicates and zero-length walls; W-43 Break Wall refusal when an opening straddles; W-44 Add Break equals Break Wall on walls; W-54 half-wall height and plan style (42 in? dashed?); W-59 Wall Hatching and Material Region behavior; W-64 curved wall radius readout and gestures; W-96 duplicate-wall merge or warning.

### 2. Wall Specification dialog and wall Edit toolbar (6)
- W-26 default Resize About per exterior/interior; W-29 thickness edit changes main layer only; W-78 Lock Center: openings keep which distance; W-83 multi-wall Open Object shows indeterminate fields; W-85 opening clamp/delete when a wall edit leaves too little room; W-90 Convert to Polyline: delete or keep the wall.

### 3. Select Objects: picking, handles, drag (19)
- Picking: S-4 CAD/text on active layer win; S-5 locked-layer objects selectable for viewing only; S-6 hover status text; S-8 right-click menu vs pan; S-29 window vs crossing marquee direction; S-30 Alt to marquee over objects; S-31 Ctrl/Cmd-click means nothing; S-32 Select Same Type UI; S-34 Tab/Shift+Tab candidate highlight; S-35 group click and double-click; S-36 Select Group Member; S-37 multi-select drag with walls is a plain translate; S-112 CAD layer order pick.
- Handles: S-11 handles with several objects selected; S-14 Shift keeps proportions; S-16 mid-edge handle on polylines; S-22 Alt free move vs Point to Point Move; S-25 text handles (built in Round 14: verify the Chief gesture); S-99 auto-scroll when dragging off screen.

## Live QA: settle these ten first

The ten open Partial, Differs-by-design or never-checked items that change the most daily output, in the order a Chief session should visit them. Each names the ids to compare and what to decide.

1. T and multi-wall joins (W-35 Differs, W-34, W-36, W-103): does Chief keep the through wall whole at a T, which wall wins at a 3-way, and are crossing walls left unbroken? Decide whether to turn `split_on_tee` off by default and build the through-wall choice (Next 25 #3).
2. Wall drawing gestures and typed entry (W-3, W-4, W-14, W-15, W-16, W-18): right-click and double-click on a chain, drag-release, the alignment guides, where the angle readout starts, Tab between length and angle, and whether Shift means 0/90 or the increment. Typed entry was built this round and has never been compared.
3. Auto Exterior Dimensions (DIM-24, DIM-33, DIM-4, DIM-40, DIM-28 Differs): string set, the 24 in spacing, an edited automatic string becoming manual, Locate defaults per exterior and interior walls, and the temporary-dimension locate group.
4. Opening placement rules (DW-13 Differs, DW-4, DW-10, DW-71, DW-6, DW-7, DW-50): the 2 in end clearance against Chief's value, whether windows may touch, alignment candidates, host picking at junctions, and the window head and exterior door defaults.
5. Door and window conventions (DW-38, DW-30, DW-43, DW-51, DW-52, DW-59, DW-60): the double-door threshold (40 in?), calculated panels, garage door default size, auto against manual mull with sidelites and transoms, 3068 shorthand and the schedule number style.
6. Roof set-up (RF-3 Missing, RF-10, RF-21, RF-25, RF-24, RF-27, RF-31, RF-32): the style preset list, overhang reference, the Dutch gable and upper-pitch field names (both flipped to Works on code evidence only), Extend Slope and Roof Return options, and how attic walls and the attic floor are named and created.
7. Room numbers that reach clients (R-49, R-51, R-52, R-42, R-26, R-40, R-13): Standard Area, what living area uses, where the readout lives, Utility default, garage drop, the Function list and the default room name.
8. Select feel (S-29, S-30, S-8, S-6, S-94, S-31, S-78 Differs): marquee direction and Alt over objects, right-click menu against pan, hover text, Ctrl-click, the modifier table, and undo depth (we keep 100, Chief defaults to 20).
9. Layout and print set-up (L-8, L-22, L-2, L-3, L-12, L-14): sheet and scale lists, Customize Sheet Sizes, Send to Layout field names and refresh modes, the line-weight multiplier and the site-plan scale bar, before the page-specification work (Next 25 #13).
10. Kitchen and stairs conventions (CB-5, CB-9, CB-13, CB-23, CB-29, CB-30, CB-34): cabinet auto-fill modifier, Ctrl disabling auto-rotation, label format (B24, W3030), default riser, tread and width, stairwell option names and the stairwell room label.

## Open items from the queue and QA files not tied to one id

- 3D: electrical devices (`plan_electrical::electrical_meshes`) and built framing (`editor/framing_view.rs`) are in the 3D view; billboards keep their stored angle; landscape uses stand-in materials (no green); curved-wall detail that stays open is listed under Walls below.
- Walls: `Wall.bottom_offset` is honored in 3D only, not in the 2D plan, room detection, elevations, schedules or wall framing (queue, manual 2.9); curved walls are mitered in plan and in 3D to the walls joined to them (straight, curved and tee), but see the open items that follow.
- Curved walls, still open: a straight wall is built square-ended in 3D (`plan_3d::wall_kinds::EndCuts::from_outline` and `build_class_cut` are ready, `plan-3d/src/lib.rs` add_wall does not pass them yet), so a straight wall meeting an arc shows a small notch or overlap at the corner; `interior_sign` in `lib.rs` probes from the chord's middle, not the arc's, so the exterior face of a strongly bowed exterior wall can flip; `Project::add_opening` clamps the center offset to the chord, not the arc length; door and window units are flat, so on a tight radius their ends sit up to about 1 1/2" off the arc on a 10' radius (inside the wall thickness); railings on an arc are built a straight piece per facet; `wall_outlines()` (the cache plan-layout reads) still gives a curved wall its chord rectangle; the pony wall's lower layers in plan are bands without mitered ends.
- Stairs: guard railings around the stairwell opening, flared apron of Flare/Curve Stairs, railing across a landing in plan, a Stair Schedule, dashed hidden treads on other floors, and stair side railings are not clipped by the floor above in 3D (queue, Round 8 notes).
- Details (chapter 17): Line Style pages are disabled, moldings now miter at polyline corners (`plan-3d/src/details.rs` test moldings_miter_at_polyline_corners), "3D Solid Feature" custom molding profile has no editor; trim does not follow walls edited through the dialog or Delete All.
- Terrain: Select Objects now has `ObjectRef::TerrainObject` (editor/selection.rs), so the manual's statement that single terrain objects cannot be picked looks stale; terrain walls and curbs now cut the TIN (done 2026-10-08, see CB-46).
- Schedules: the placed tables are not in the DXF export or the construction-set PDF; the table is rebuilt every frame; Cabinet/Plant/Fixture rows come from placed symbols.
- Doc drift to correct in the manual: chapter 2.4 says the Fix Wall Connections button is not wired (it is: `editor/actions.rs`, `editor/connect.rs`); chapter 3 says pointer-derived swing/hinge is planned (fixed as QA-01); chapter 10.10 says 3D picking is planned for Round 9 (it is built: `shell/view3d_panel/pick.rs`, scenarios s24).

## Coverage audit (2026-10-08, Round 14)

The Chief X18 feature coverage audit (`docs/chief-feature-coverage.md`) inventoried 1099 Chief features and found 195 with no parity row. The rows below were appended to the matching `docs/parity/*.md` files (section "Coverage audit additions") and are repeated here in the per-id format. **The totals table at the top of this file was not changed**; the orchestrator recounts at the gate.

Recount note: the totals table counts 765 ids (W, S, DW, R, RF, DIM, TXT, CAD, LAY, C, CB, L). Round 14 also created ids outside that table: E-1..E-16, PR-1..PR-16, HK-1..HK-7 and TB-1..TB-5 (44 ids, in `docs/parity/electrical.md` and `docs/parity/preferences-hotkeys-toolbars.md`). This audit adds 195 ids: APP- 66, C- 8, CAD- 16, CB- 21, DIM- 1, DW- 13, E- 1, L- 9, LAY- 22, R- 17, RF- 1, S- 5, TXT- 2, W- 13. Status of the added rows: Works 98, Partial 25, Missing 67, Differs-by-design 5. APP- is a new series for application-shell features (files, windows, help, Tools menu) that had no home in the existing specs; its first id is APP-1 and it lives in `docs/parity/preferences-hotkeys-toolbars.md`.

## Manual audit (part 2), 2026-10-08

Rows from the Reference Manual audit of pages 319 to 520 (`docs/chief-manual-coverage/part2-cad-walls-rooms-dimensions.md`), appended to the matching `docs/parity/*.md` files under "Manual audit additions (part 2)". The totals table above is not changed.

| Id | Area | Behavior | Status | Evidence | Gap |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CAD-107 | CAD, images and details | CAD Defaults dialog (per view) (manual p. 319): One CAD Defaults dialog per floor level, per cross section or elevation view, per CAD detail and wall detail and per layout file, holding the Current CAD Layer, the Displayed Line Length format, the line-angle display style and Show Arc Centers and Ends; not available in 3D camera views. | Missing | no CAD Defaults dialog; Active Layers by Tool (dialogs/layer_sets.rs) sets the Current CAD Layer, ViewFlag::ArcCenters the arc centers; the Default Settings cad.* pages (default_pages/cad.rs, 0 bound fields) are stored and not read by tools/cad.rs | Added by the manual audit (part 2), 2026-10-08. |
| CAD-108 | CAD, images and details | Displayed Line Length format (manual p. 320): Format of the length numbers CAD lines and arcs can show: units, unit indicators, leading and trailing zeros, thousands separator, inches below a length, fraction style and text size, decimal places or smallest fraction (show denominator, reduce, GCD or closest), with a preview. | Missing | no CAD length indicators exist; plan-core dimension.rs DimFormat/LengthFormat already holds the same fields for dimensions (DIM-6) and could back it | Added by the manual audit (part 2), 2026-10-08. |
| CAD-109 | CAD, images and details | Display Line Angles As (degrees, quadrant bearings, azimuth bearings) (manual pp. 320-321): How the angle of a CAD line is shown and typed: degrees/minutes/seconds counter-clockwise from east, quadrant bearings (N 20d 30' E) or azimuth bearings; used by Show Angle and by the New CAD Line and Arc dialogs for survey (plot plan) work. | Missing | no bearing parsing or display anywhere in plan-app/plan-core (grep bearing finds only unrelated crates) | Added by the manual audit (part 2), 2026-10-08. |
| CAD-110 | CAD, images and details | Revision Cloud Defaults and multiple saved defaults (manual pp. 319-320): Revision Cloud Defaults dialog sets the initial cloud look and layer (clouds do not use the Current CAD Layer); revision clouds support several saved defaults and default sets. | Missing | no revision cloud defaults page (default_pages/cad.rs has none); the tool uses a fixed 12 in scallop, tools/cad.rs CLOUD_ARC | Added by the manual audit (part 2), 2026-10-08. |
| CAD-111 | CAD, images and details | Polyline Specification panels (Polyline report, Spline, Selected Line/Arc, Visibility, Label, Components, Object Information, Schedule) (manual p. 354): The Polyline dialog reports count, length, perimeter, area, volume and holes, has a Spline panel (New Segment Angle 0-45 degrees), a Selected Line or Selected Arc panel for the selected edge with Show Selected Edge, Label, Components, Object Information and Schedule panels (CAD polylines can be listed in schedules). | Partial | dialogs/cad.rs shows Perimeter and Area (CAD-26) and vertex list; no hole count, volume, spline segment angle, selected-edge panel, label, components, object information or schedule | Added by the manual audit (part 2), 2026-10-08. |

## Manual audit (part 5), 2026-10-08

Rows from the Reference Manual audit of pages 1099 to 1306 (`docs/chief-manual-coverage/part5-materials-3d-rendering-pictures-import-export.md`), appended to the matching `docs/parity/*.md` files under "Manual audit additions (part 5)". The totals table above is not changed.

| Id | Area | Behavior | Status | Evidence | Gap |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C-80 | 3D/Cameras | Keep Pattern/Texture in Sync and Global Symbol Mapping (manual p. 1100): Keep Pattern/Texture in Sync: one check box ties scale, offset and angle of pattern and texture together. | Missing | the Pattern and Texture tabs hold separate scale and angle (`pattern_scale`, `texture_scale_in`) | Added by the manual audit (part 5), 2026-10-08. |
| C-81 | 3D/Cameras | Stretch to Fit textures and Retain Aspect Ratio on the Texture tab (manual pp. 1100-1121): Stretch to Fit textures (artwork that does not tile and is stretched across the surface, for picture frames); Texture Scale X and Y, Stretch to Fit, Retain Aspect Ratio, Reset Original Aspect Ratio. | Partial | Texture tab has only a tile size; no stretch flag / Tile size width and height (C-61b); no Stretch to Fit, no aspect lock, no reset | Added by the manual audit (part 5), 2026-10-08. |
| C-82 | 3D/Cameras | Material maps beyond the PBR set (anisotropy, rotation, emissive, translucent, transmission, transparent) (manual pp. 1100-1126): Fourteen material map kinds: ambient occlusion, anisotropy, bump, emissive, metal, normal, opacity, rotation, roughness, texture, translucent, transmission color, transmission roughness, transparent; Material Class Mix for General materials: Metal Map, Translucent Map with Translucency, Transmission Color Map, Transmission Roughness (+map), Transparent Map, Index of Refraction. | Partial | normal, roughness, metallic, height (bump), ambient occlusion and opacity maps from a package (BC-PKG1, BC-PKG6, BC-PKG7, BC-PKG8) plus a scalar bump; anisotropy, rotation, emissive, translucent, transmission and transparent maps are not read (BC-PKG11) / see the maps row | Added by the manual audit (part 5), 2026-10-08. |
| C-83 | 3D/Cameras | Per-map Invert and Remove on the Texture and Properties tabs (manual p. 1101): Invert check box next to each map (all but texture, normal, ambient occlusion and transmission color). | Missing | only `MaterialDef.normal_flip_y` for a DirectX normal map; no per-map invert | Added by the manual audit (part 5), 2026-10-08. |
| C-84 | 3D/Cameras | Emissive materials as area lights in ray-traced views (manual p. 1101): Emissive materials act as Area Lights in ray-traced views, and a fixture's bulb material can take its color and intensity from the fixture's own lights. | Partial | `plan-render` `AreaLight` panels are sampled directly and can be set in the Ray Trace window, and an Emissive class material glows (C-61c); the plan's Emissive materials do not become area lights, and there is no "Apply Emissive and Color from Light(s)" switch | Added by the manual audit (part 5), 2026-10-08. |

## Manual audit (part 6), 2026-10-08

Rows from the Reference Manual audit of pages 1307 to 1497 (`docs/chief-manual-coverage/part6-terrain-plants-materials-lists-layout-printing-schedules.md`), appended to the matching `docs/parity/*.md` files under "Manual audit additions (part 6)". The totals table above is not changed by these rows.

| Id | Area | Behavior | Status | Evidence | Gap |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CB-505 | Cabinets, stairs, framing, terrain, library | Terrain Perimeter appears ready-made (manual p. 1308): 50 ft x 100 ft in an empty plan, grown to enclose any 3D model already drawn, Fill Window to see it | Partial | ours is clicked point by point; `Terrain::default` is a 100 ft x 80 ft lot but the tool never uses it; no auto-size to the building footprint (tools/terrain.rs, plan-terrain model.rs) | Added by the manual audit (part 6), 2026-10-08. |
| CB-506 | Cabinets, stairs, framing, terrain, library | Terrain elevations are absolute (sea level), independent of the Floor 1 height, so real survey elevations can be used with floors still starting at 0 (manual p. 1309) | Partial | plan-terrain keeps absolute inches and `site_view` ties the pad to the first floor; no explicit sea-level model or Absolute Elevation switch | Added by the manual audit (part 6), 2026-10-08. |

## Manual audit (part 7), 2026-10-08

Rows from the Tutorial Guide audit (pages 1 to 517, `docs/chief-manual-coverage/part7-tutorial-workflows.md`), appended to the matching `docs/parity/*.md` files under "Manual audit additions (part 7)". The totals table above is not changed by these rows.

| Id | Area | Behavior | Status | Evidence | Gap |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| W-150 | Walls | Wall type properties that steer other tools (tutorial pp. 11, 19): per wall type, the layer a foundation wall bears under (Foundation to Exterior of Layer) and the layer dimensions locate (Dimension to Exterior of Layer, on the Wall Properties tab of Wall Type Definitions). | Missing | WallTypeDef holds layers and kind only (plan-core defaults.rs); dimensions locate the main layer or the surfaces by the dimension defaults (DIM-4), not by a per-type layer; no foundation-bearing layer setting. | Added by the manual audit (part 7), 2026-10-08. |
| W-151 | Walls | Build > Wall > Define Wall Types opens Wall Type Definitions straight from the menu, and Copy starts a new type from the current one (tutorial pp. 9, 48, 353). | Partial | dialogs/wall_types.rs opens only through Define in a Wall Specification or Wall Defaults; menus.rs has no wall-types entry; New Type copies the current type as '<name> copy n'. | Added by the manual audit (part 7), 2026-10-08. |
| W-152 | Walls | Room Divider as a wall type: switching a wall to the Room Divider type drops its thickness to 0 in and shows it as a dashed pair of lines; a divider is 0 in thick by default (tutorial p. 39). | Missing | a divider is a wall class/flag with its own layer (plan-core walls.rs ROOM_DIVIDER_LAYER, WallVariant::RoomDivider) and keeps a wall thickness; the template has no Room Divider wall type. | Added by the manual audit (part 7), 2026-10-08. |
| W-153 | Walls | Wall Types panel preview you can drag to rotate, with a Vector View drop-down that switches to Show Plan View (tutorial pp. 8, 37, 52). | Partial | dialogs/wall.rs shows a layer preview; no rotation and no plan/vector/3D selector (APP-86 covers dialog previews in general). | Added by the manual audit (part 7), 2026-10-08. |
| W-154 | Walls | Dragging a wall end along a railing or room divider replaces that stretch of railing with the wall, because two walls cannot share a place (tutorial pp. 74-75, 443). | Missing | a wall drawn or dragged over another makes crossing walls or merges (W-36 Partial); there is no replace-on-drag rule. | Added by the manual audit (part 7), 2026-10-08. |
| W-155 | Walls | Half Wall defaults that are both Railing and Pony Wall: upper and lower wall types, Elevation of Lower Wall Top, and a Height that sets only the upper wall (tutorial p. 109). | Partial | `wall_variants` holds half_wall_height and pony_split_height as separate variants (plan-app assets/templates/chief-x18-daniel.json); one wall that is both: not found. | Added by the manual audit (part 7), 2026-10-08. |
| R-143 | Rooms and floors | Floor/Ceiling Platform Defaults page (tutorial pp. 13, 178, 204): Floor Structure, Floor Finish, Ceiling Structure and Ceiling Finish Edit buttons that open layered definitions every floor and room type can follow. | Missing | dialogs/floor_defaults.rs keeps two thicknesses per floor and dialogs/room.rs keeps a layer stack per room (R-28); no platform defaults page (related R-122, R-124). | Added by the manual audit (part 7), 2026-10-08. |
| R-144 | Rooms and floors | Structure definition fields (tutorial pp. 13, 111, 182): per layer Framing Method (joists), Construction (Lumber, I-Joist, Hat Channel), Width, Spacing and Role, so the joist layer drives the framing and a layer can be changed from I-Joist to 2x12 lumber. | Missing | StructureLayer is material plus thickness (dialogs/room.rs define_editor); plan-framing takes joist size and spacing from Framing Defaults (CB-220), not from the structure. | Added by the manual audit (part 7), 2026-10-08. |
| R-145 | Rooms and floors | Room Supplies Floor for the Room Above (tutorial p. 50): a foundation-level room whose slab and curbs become the floor of the room above (a garage on a slab). | Missing | no such flag on rooms (dialogs/room.rs Structure tab); R-115 lists other Structure fields still missing. | Added by the manual audit (part 7), 2026-10-08. |
